## U1 decisions (statistics: WP-02b statistics API for the other consumers), 2026-11-03

Published early so the reports / export / analysis / rankings / human_eval re-points can use the names (details follow in the final statistics entry):

- `statistics.services.study.study_jobs` → **`study_runs(user=None, *, study_id=None, run_ids=None, current_only=True, stage="main")`** (QuerySet[GenerationRun]; with `study_id` it is `runs.view.current_runs(study, stage)`, else per-cell first-usable selection over the user's runs; `beyond_plan` always excluded; owner-scoped unless staff/None).
- `job_labels(job)` / `JOB_LABEL_KEYS` → **`run_labels(run)` / `RUN_LABEL_KEYS`** = `run_id, model, model_name, provider, kind, stack, task, unit_key, condition_id, condition_label, repeat_index` (`template`→`task`, `app_id`→`unit_key`, `mode`→`kind`, `build_healing` dropped). `app_unit(row)` = `(condition_id, model, kind, unit_key or run_id)`.
- `condition_label(condition)` stays (StudyCondition `label`, else the model id); `code_metrics_by_job` → **`code_metrics_by_run(result_ids)`** (keys are GenerationRun ids). `issue_breakdowns(runs, ...)`, `group_summary`, `tool_coverage`, `dynamic_slugs`, `SEVERITIES`, `CODE_METRIC_*` keep their names. `healing_impact`, `HEALING_PER_APP_KEYS` and the `healing_ablation` command are **gone**.
- Row keys: per-app rows carry `run_id` (GenerationRun) and `analysis_run_id` (was `run_id`); issue clusters (`IssueCluster.to_dict`) carry `run_id` (was `job_id`). `manifest_unused_scaffold` → `manifest_unused_starter`; new per-app columns `loc_app`, `code_unique_model` (code issues in model-scope files) — `loc` is model-scope source LOC (Appendix A #4) and `unique_per_kloc` = `code_unique_model` per model kLOC; `loc` is `None` (never 0) for a run without a collected tree.
- `statistics.services.dependencies`: `scaffold_specs`/`scaffold_packages`/`scaffold_spec_affected` → **`starter_specs(run, memo)`/`starter_packages`/`starter_spec_affected`**; `job_files` → **`run_files(run)`** (app scope, non-source dirs dropped); `dependency_ownership(run, memo=None, *, files=None)` (pass `files` from `run_views_code(runs, "app")` in bulk); `DependencyOwnership.scaffold` → `.starter`. Pure helpers (`manifest_specs`, `normalize_package`, `_same_spec`, `spec_range`, `lock_*`, `cluster_package`) unchanged.
- `statistics.services.accounting`: `run_accounting(runs)`; the `apps` block counts `runs`/`generated`/`analyzed`/`evaluated`, evaluations come from the latest `RunEvaluation` per run (status counts; `not_measured`/`error` are not "evaluated").

## U1 decisions (analysis: WP-02b), 2026-11-03

- **App scope only.** `AnalysisRun.get_code_for_analysis()` returns `run_view(run).code_files("app")` for a generation run (inline `source_code` unchanged). This is the one edit inside `analysis/models.py` that WP-01 granted. The runner gives the tools the app tree verbatim (non-source dirs left out as before; real paths are never renamed, `_materialize` applies to pasted code only, `runner.analysis_files`). At the start of a run it records `code_scope="app"` and `code_scope_paths_sha = identity.H(sorted analyzed paths)`.
- **Origin tagging without a column.** Each finding stores its file's origin in `Finding.tool_specific_data["file_origin"]` (`runner.FILE_ORIGIN_KEY`): `starter|authored|modified|slot`, or `parent` for a slot variant's overlaid parent file, taken from `RunView.files("app")`. The key is absent when the path is not a tree file (a DAST URL, a package-only advisory, pasted code), and attribution must treat that as unattributed, never as model code. The API exposes `file_origin` on findings and `origin` on `source_files`. No consumer reads `tool_specific_data` wholesale (dedup and export read single keys), so fingerprints do not change.
- **`not_measured` replaces the "true zero".** A tool with no applicable in-scope file is still stored as `status=skipped, skip_reason=not_applicable` (rows stay comparable), but its summary now carries `tool_applicable: false, measurement: "not_measured"`. `runner._is_measurement` and `measured_scopes` no longer count it, so a scope covered only by not-applicable tools is `None`, not 0, in the run summary, facets and `run_scope_counts`. The context prevalence denominators (`apps_measured`) count completed results only. Ran tools get `tool_applicable: true, measurement: "measured"` (failed: `not_measured`, `tool_applicable` kept). The run summary has `tool_applicable: {slug: true|false|null}` (null means not decided: cancelled, unavailable, app not running). `rerun?only_unmeasured` still skips not-applicable tools, because the tree is immutable.
- **Per-tool latest unchanged.** `ToolResult.latest_ids_per_job` and `AnalysisRun.latest_ids_per_job` are untouched. Not-applicable still ranks as "decided" over a later failure, so selection is the same as in v2; only the counting changed (above). A test pins this.
- **Truncation.** `summary.truncated_inputs` is `[validity.termination]` when `GenerationRun.validity.truncated` is true (v2: backend/frontend/agent flags from the old per-job JSON).
- **`AnalysisRun.batch_id` is renamed `launch_id`**, column included (U1 fixer round 1: the earlier state-only rename kept `db_column="batch_id"`, which the gate grep flags), in `analysis/migrations/0011_v3_launch_id.py` (`RenameField`, a real column rename). The reason is that the U1 gate grep forbids `batch_id`. API changes: `POST runs/batch/` now returns `{launch_id, run_ids}`, `GET runs/?launch_id=` replaces `?batch_id=`, and run payloads carry `launch_id`, `code_scope` and `code_scope_paths_sha`.
- **API shapes (WP-17).**
  - The request fields `generation_job_id(s)` keep their name; they are GenerationRun ids, matching the FK attribute (D5).
  - `target.kind` is `"run"` (was `"job"`). Its fields are `job_id, job_status, outcome, run_kind, model_name, model_id, task_title, task_slug, task_ref, stack_ref, study_id`; the old `mode, app_name, app_slug, stack_slug` and the v2 study-id key are gone. List views build every `RunView` in one query.
  - Context `scope` values are `study|mine` (the v2 value is gone). `job` (RunSetupSchema) is `{id, kind, status, outcome, usable, model_*, task_ref, task_slug, task_title, prose_variant, unit_key, stack_ref, study_id, study_name, condition_id, condition_label, stage, repeat_index, planned_repeats (= Study.repeats), harness, harness_version, config_fingerprint, run_fingerprint}`.
  - `source_loc` counts the **model** scope (`scope: "model"`; pasted code: `"code"`) and adds `app_total` (source LOC of the analyzed app tree).
- **Corpora.** `study` is `current_runs(study, stage="main")` (first usable attempt per cell; beyond_plan runs out). `mine` is the caller's runs outside any cell plus `current_runs` of each of their studies (pilot and main), so later attempts of a cell are never counted. `interim` is set while `Study.status` is not completed, cancelled or stopped.
- **Shop coverage.** `coverage.app_coverage` reads the app scope of the user's 200 most recent completed runs with a tree, through `run_views_code(runs, "app")`.
- **Temporary test settings** `backend/analysis/tests/_tmp_u1_settings.py` + `_tmp_u1_urls.py` (DELETE at the U1 gate). They load `statistics.services` without its v2 `__init__` and mount only the analysis routers. Under them the analysis suite and `u1_runview` pass (366). Under `config.settings.test` the API tests fail at URLconf import, inside other apps' modules, until those are re-pointed.

## U1 asks (analysis)

- **statistics / reports / human_eval / export / generation `api/job_insights.py` owners**: `skip_reason == "not_applicable"` is **not** a measurement in v3 (spec 8.8: `not_measured`, never 0). Please stop treating it as "measured / a true zero" in `statistics/services/{study,aggregations,similarity}.py`, `reports/services/{generators,study_report}.py`, `human_eval/services/snapshot.py`, `generation/api/job_insights.py`, and in their tests that assert the zero. The per-result flag is `summary.tool_applicable is False` (or the skip reason, for rows from before it existed). `runner.SKIP_NOT_APPLICABLE`, `runner.NOT_MEASURED` and `runner.FILE_ORIGIN_KEY` are the shared constants. Model/slot attribution at stats time should read `Finding.tool_specific_data["file_origin"]`; an absent key means unattributed.
- **WP-01 / lead**: consider a real `Finding.file_origin` column (Char(12), indexed with `result`) and a `ToolResult.tool_applicable` column to replace the JSON keys above; both can be filled from the JSON in a data migration.
- **WP-17 (frontend)**: `lib/api/runs.ts` and the analysis pages should use `launch_id` (not the v2 key), `target.kind === "run"` and the new target/setup fields, context `scope=study`, `source_loc.scope`/`app_total`, and `file_origin` on findings.
- **Lead / statistics owner**: `backend/statistics/services/__init__.py` still imports v2 models (`aggregations.py`), and `config/api.py` mounts routers whose modules import removed models (`llm_models`, `reports`, …). The analysis views import `statistics.services.dedup` and `reports.services.loc`, so they import cleanly only once those packages do.

## U1 decisions (misc_apps: WP-02b llm_models, rankings, system, users, automation), 2026-11-03

- **rankings** `aggregate_rankings(user, *, prompt_hash, config_fingerprint, study_id)` (was `bundle_key`/`experiment_id`; `GET /rankings/?study_id=&config_fingerprint=`, 400 on a malformed `study_id`). Runs with `beyond_plan=True` are never counted. The repeat-collapsing unit is `(condition_id, task_version_id)`. LOC is model scope: `metrics["loc_model"]`, else `RunView.loc("model")`. A run whose LOC is `None` (no collected tree) is **not measured**. Code findings count only when `Finding.file_path` is in the run's `RunView.files("model")`, so starter and parent files are skipped. Dependency advisories and dynamic-tool findings are classified before that filter, as before. Pass rate is intention-to-treat: a usable `build_fail`/`start_fail`/`output_invalid` run counts 0; a `tested` run counts `metrics["eval"].agent_pass_rate` only when `measured`; anything else is ungraded (None). Still uses `ToolResult.latest_ids_per_job` and `statistics.services.study.tool_coverage`, the latter imported per call along with `reports.services.loc.is_test_path`.
- **llm_models** insights read `GenerationRun` + `run_views`. Per-run stats: cost is `metrics.cost_usd`; LLM calls are the sum of `requests_by_class`; LOC is `loc_model`, else `view.loc("model")`; harness is `view.harness`; termination is `result.termination`; providers come from `RunAttempt.served.providers`; functional comes from `metrics["eval"]` (success = measured with `agent_pass_rate >= 1`). The insights payload keeps its key names (`jobs_total`, `by_mode` = counts per run kind, `experiments` = the studies list, `engines`), so the model page keeps working. New key: `by_outcome`. `repair_rate` and `continuations_used` are `None`, since v3 has neither. The template breakdown is grouped per `task_version__task__slug`. Sampling usage (`/models/detail/{slug}/sampling/`) reads the manifest's `sampling` block. Only `provider_default` counts as "known not sent"; `explicit` or a missing manifest counts as unknown. `harness_sampling_stripped` items are `{key, jobs, calls: None}`. Presets come from `run.sampling_preset`. `LLMModel` related lookups are `generation_runs`. `import_external_covariates`: `--job` → `--run` and a new `--study`.
- **system** `clear_stuck_generation_jobs` fails stuck runs as `status=failed, outcome=infra_error, outcome_reason="maintenance_stuck", usable=False`. The row update is the important part: it then calls `execution.transitions.publish_run(run)` and `maybe_complete_study(study_id)`, both best effort with logged exceptions. Orphan sandbox reaping calls `generation.agentic.sandbox.reap_orphans(older_than_seconds=)` and counts 0 until WP-08 ports it. The `app-stats` key `generation_jobs` is kept for the system page and now counts GenerationRun.
- **users** `/users/me/activity/`: counts `studies` (was `experiments`); recent items have kind `study` (href `/studies/{id}`); sample items keep kind `application`, with href `/samples/{id}` and title `task · model` from `RunView`.
- **automation** `dispatch_generate` takes a `design` (contracts.plan.Design) from the step or pipeline params and calls `study.launch.launch_adhoc(design, user=, launch=True)`. It then polls the created runs until all are terminal (`timeout_seconds`, default 6 h). Output: `study_id, plan_hash, generation_run_ids, generation_run_id (first), run_statuses`, status `completed` if at least one run completed. The analyze step reads `generation_run_id`. The automation-own `Batch` API param was renamed `batch_pk`, and `BatchItemSchema` drops the duplicate `batch_id` (`batch` stays). The automation router is still unmounted.

They pass (273 passed, 29 skipped) with a temporary URLconf that mounts only the users/models/rankings/system routers. That URLconf was deleted after the run. Re-run once reports imports.

When WP-10 replaces `current_runs` with `runs.queries.current_runs` the import follows `runs.view`.
- **Infra (all agents):** the shared `llmlab-postgres-1` has `/dev/shm` at 96% (64 MB default); every `.wt-test.sh` run fails at test-DB setup with `DiskFull: could not resize shared memory segment`. I did not touch it; I verified with a private throwaway postgres (`--shm-size=256m`, same image, stopped after). The lead should raise its shm_size or have test DBs dropped.

## U1 decisions (integrations_realtime, WP-02b part), 2026-11-03

- **Webhook labels** (`integrations/services/webhooks.py`): `RUN_LABEL = "generation.GenerationRun"`, `STUDY_LABEL = "generation.Study"`. Under `config.settings.test`, `django.setup()` now succeeds (the `integrations` `ready()` no longer names v2 models). `tests/wp01_foundation/settings_u1.py` is not needed for integrations any more; the lead can delete it at the gate. `test_every_label_resolves_through_apps_get_model` checks every label in `_STATUS_EVENTS`, `_SWEEP_TIME_FIELDS` and `_PAYLOAD_BUILDERS` through `apps.get_model`, and checks the status values, time fields and `created_by`.
- **Event ids are renamed (clean break):** `generation.{completed,failed,cancelled}` become `run.*`, and `experiment.{completed,cancelled}` become `study.*`. `study.stopped` is new (StudyStatus.STOPPED). Payload `object.type` is `generation_run` / `study`, and the URLs are `/samples/<run>` and `/studies/<study>`. The analysis payload's `data.generation_job_id` is now `data.run_id`. The run payload reads `run_view()` (task title/ref, model slug, cost, tokens). A pass rate appears only when `metrics.eval.measured` holds; otherwise `data.eval_status` is the eval status, or `not_measured` when there is no evaluation, and the "Pass rate" field shows that status. It is never 0.
- **`WebhookSubscription.include_experiment_jobs` becomes `include_study_runs`** (API field too). Run events for runs with a `study_id` reach only subscriptions that opt in. Ad-hoc runs belong to a one-run study, and `study.completed` covers them. Migration `integrations/0002_v3_study_run_events.py` (own app) does RenameField + AlterField and rewrites stored event names in `WebhookSubscription.events` (reversible).
- **GitHub export** (`integrations/services/github.py`, `api/github.py`): the v2 `include_scaffold` flag is replaced by `scope: "app" | "model"` (default `app`). Files come from `RunView.files(scope)` with raw blob bytes, and the exec bit from `RunFile.mode`. A missing blob lands in `skipped`, never exported empty. Gists always use `model`. The README provenance shows task (pinned ref), stack, harness, style, study, config fingerprint and run id. `application_id` is kept as the request key (it is the run UUID).
- **MCP** (`integrations/mcp/tools.py`): `Curated` gains `enabled` / `pending`. The v2 generation, template and experiment tools are removed. These study and run tools are registered **disabled**: `list_studies`, `get_study`, `resolve_study`, `launch_study` (pending WP-10) and `list_runs`, `get_run` (pending WP-08). Their `operation_id` is `""`, and disabled tools are neither listed nor callable. `tools.pending_tools()` lists them. **To enable one:** set `operation_id` to the published ninja id (without `backend_`) and drop `enabled=False`. `test_every_enabled_curated_tool_resolves_to_an_operation` then guards it. The server instructions and prompts now describe studies (`experiment_report` becomes `study_report`).
- **Realtime:** `events.run_channel`, `events.study_channel`, `publish_run_event(run_id, event)` and `publish_study_event(study_id, event)` validate against `contracts.events` (`parse_run_event` / `parse_study_event`). A malformed event is logged and not published. `experiment_channel` and `publish_experiment_event` are removed. In `authz.py`, `run:<uuid>` is allowed for staff, the run's creator, or the creator of its study. `study:<uuid>` is allowed for the study's creator or staff. `generation:` and `experiment:` are refused. Unknown ids are refused for staff too.
- **Tests deleted** (they pinned removed behaviour): `test_github.py::test_scaffold_failure_falls_back_to_raw_files` and `test_export_repo_uses_blobs_for_binary`, which was rewritten as `test_export_repo_app_scope_is_the_buildable_tree`. The others were ported.

## U1 decisions (inference_runtime: WP-02a inference + runtime re-point), 2026-11-03

Files: `inference/{api/views.py,chat.py,proxy_server.py}`, `runtime/{api/views.py,services/container_service.py,services/smoke.py}`, tests in `inference/tests/` and `runtime/tests/{test_api,test_smoke,test_sample_builds,test_sample_build_integration}.py`. No migrations, no model changes.

- **Proxy auth reads `GenerationRun` + `RunManifest`.** `AuthCache.get` loads the run with `select_related("manifest", "model__endpoint")`. `JobAuth` now has `endpoint` (frozen section), `model_slug` (`manifest.model.slug`) and `wall_clock_s` instead of the v2 `snapshot`. The `job_*` names are kept because they follow `LLMRequest.job`. Budget logic is unchanged (WP-06).
- **`proxy_server.frozen_endpoint(manifest, model)`:** the manifest's `model.endpoint` (id, kind, served_model_name, digest) is authoritative. The keys the v3 manifest does not freeze (`num_ctx`, `keep_alive`, `api_flavor`, `capabilities`; `ManifestEndpoint` is `extra=forbid`) come from `inference.snapshot.endpoint_section(run.model)`. Without that fallback, a local run would silently get the Ollama server's default context. It returns None (proxy answers 401) for OpenRouter endpoints and for runs without a manifest.
- **Token lifetime:** `proxy_server.manifest_wall_clock_s(manifest)` reads `time.wall_clock_s` from `manifest.resources.effective`. Three shapes are accepted: an `EffectivePolicy` dump under `content`, a bare content dict, or the flat knob key. With no value it falls back to the unchanged v2 `DEFAULT_WALL_CLOCK_S` (3600), never 0.
- `record_infra_error` and `chat.add_queue_wait` update `generation_run` (`GenerationRun._meta.db_table`). Cancel checks use `RunStatus.CANCELLED`.
- **`chat.chat(..., source=...)`: `source` is now required.** v3 has no default caller kind. The only production caller (`analysis/services/ai_runner.py`) already passes `"analysis"`.
- `inference/api/views.py`: the active-run counts and the delete-endpoint "in use" check read `GenerationRun` and `StudyCondition.model__endpoint`.
- **Runtime:** `container_service.build_for_job(run, ...)` keeps its name (human_eval hosting already calls it with a run) and builds the run's app tree through `runtime.services.build_context` (`sample_builds` → `prepare_build_dir`).
  - The container metadata no longer records the always-`none` build-context report (formerly the `healing` key). It keeps `build` metrics.
  - The runtime API routes keep the `/runtime/jobs/{job_id}/{build,smoke}/` paths for the current frontend; `job_id` is a GenerationRun id. There were no Stack views left in `runtime/api`.
- **`smoke.run_smoke(run, container)`** probes the GET endpoints of `run_view(run).task_dict()["structured"]["endpoints"]` (`auth == "admin"` marks admin routes) and stores the result **only** in `ContainerInstance.metadata["smoke"]`. It no longer writes `GenerationRun.metrics`: `RunMetrics` is `extra=forbid`, and Appendix A #7 forbids smoke checks from overwriting metrics.
- **Temporary:** `backend/inference/tests/u1ir_tmp/{settings,urls}.py` is a URLconf with only the runtime, inference, relay and app-proxy routes. The full URLconf still fails to import through the v2 consumers in llm_models/statistics/users (WP-02b). Delete it at the U1 gate. Run the area with `.wt-test.sh u1ir -- pytest --ds=backend.inference.tests.u1ir_tmp.settings backend/inference/tests backend/runtime/tests`.
- **Tests deleted** (they pinned removed behaviour):
  - `test_e2e_relay.py::{test_executor_runs_a_scaffolding_experiment, test_executor_job_survives_a_relay_restart, test_executor_cancel_aborts_the_upstream_call}` (the v2 experiment launch API and scaffolding mode; the v3 executor E2E is WP-08/WP-11, 13.5);
  - `test_gateway_wiring.py::{test_sandbox_names_carry_the_attempt, test_sandbox_starts_the_local_gateway_with_its_wiring}` (v2 `AgentSandbox`);
  - `test_snapshot_local.py::{test_openrouter_snapshot_unchanged, test_local_snapshot, test_harness_rendering_gets_the_client_timeout, test_scaffolding_fingerprint}` (v2 agent snapshot builders and `profile_resolver`). The file now tests `endpoint_section`/`llm_source`, `frozen_endpoint` and the v3 weights-in-fingerprint rule through `identity.compute_keys`;
  - `test_sample_build_integration.py::test_evaluation_and_two_fresh_previews_build_only_once`, rewritten as `test_two_fresh_previews_build_the_run_tree_only_once`. The shared evaluation image belongs to WP-09;
  - `test_smoke.py::test_smoke_keeps_conformance_verdict`, replaced by `test_smoke_never_touches_run_metrics`.

## U1 decisions (human_eval: WP-02b), 2026-11-03

- **Reads go through `RunView` only.** `snapshot.py`, `pool.py`, `context.py`, `source.py`, `hosting.py` and `api/views/portal_work.py` read runs through `generation.runs.view` (`run_view`, `files`/`code_files("app")`, `loc`, `latest_evaluation`, `task_dict`, `current_runs`). `EvalTaskApp.job` is the app's `GenerationRun` (FK attribute kept, D5). Participants see, and hosting builds, the **app** scope: the whole tree they test.
- **Snapshot keys** (`EvalTaskApp.snapshot`, pinned by `tests/test_pool.py::test_snapshot_keys_are_stable_for_a_factory_run`). Light snapshot: `run_id, model_id, model_name, provider, condition_id, condition_label, study_id, study_name, app_slug (task slug), app_name (task title), task_ref, prose_variant, stack, auth_mode, unit_key, repeat_index, kind, harness, functional, evaluator_version (EvalSuite ref), tester_version, created_at, spec_content_hash (= run.task_hash), run_fingerprint`. First open adds `checks, static_findings, blinding_scan` and `spec` (= `RunView.task_dict()`). The v2 keys `experiment_id/_name, mode, tester_sha256` are gone.
- **`functional = {status, pass_rate, deployed, source}`** comes from `RunView.latest_evaluation()`. `source` is `"eval_suite"` (was `conformance`/`smoke`). `pass_rate` is `metrics.agent_pass_rate` and is set **only** for a `measured` evaluation whose result says `measured: true`. `status` is `tested`, the evaluation status (`pending`, `running`, `not_measured`, `error`), or the run outcome (`build_fail`, `start_fail`, `infra_error`, ...) when the app was never deployed or evaluated. `deployed` comes from the start stage (`ok` gives True, `fail` gives False), and is False for build/start-fail outcomes. Missing data is None, never 0.
- **Endpoint checks.** `checks = [{name: "METHOD /path", category: "api"|"admin", passed, endpoint_id}]` holds one entry per task endpoint that the tester checked. It counts only `pass`/`fail` checks that are not flaky, and an endpoint passes only when all of its counted checks pass. Endpoints without a counted check are left out (not measured). `functional.by_category` is the tester's `metrics.by_category` as `{passed, total}`.
- **Static findings.** `{count, loc, loc_model, tools, by_tool}`. `loc` is app-scope source LOC (analysis runs on the app tree) and `loc_model` is the model scope. Either is None without a collected tree. Following the analysis ask, a not-applicable tool (skipped, or `tool_applicable: false`) is **not** a measurement: it is left out of `tools`, never counted as a zero.
- **Requirement sources are now `frontend` (the task's `ui` requirements), `api` (non-admin endpoints) and `admin_api` (endpoints with `auth == "admin"`).** The v2 `backend`/`admin` prose sources have no v3 counterpart and were removed from the design vocabulary (`design.REQUIREMENT_SOURCES`, presets, analysis prefix map). An old design that names them fails validation (clean break). Endpoint item text is `"METHOD /path"`. The participant spec view (`WorkAppSpecView`) is `{name (title), description (summary), prose (the variant the model got), frontend_requirements, api_endpoints, admin_api_endpoints}`.
- **Admin credentials (no `ADMIN_PW_DEFAULT`).** `services/credentials.admin_credentials(run)` calls `backend.generation.evaluation.credentials(run)` (WP-09 contract, spec 8.6). It shows `{username: record.admin_username or "admin", password: record.admin_password}`. If the record is missing or unreadable, or the stub raises `NotImplementedError`, there is no credential: `admin_credentials: null` with the new field `admin_credentials_status: "not_measured"` (`"available"` when shown; null when the blinding setting hides it). There is never a default password. Tests monkeypatch `evaluation.credentials`.
- **Candidates and adding.** Candidates are completed `app_agentic`/slot runs (never `reference`, never `beyond_plan`). `current_only` applies `current_runs`' rule as a queryset filter: the first usable attempt of a cell, else its latest attempt (attempt order is `created_at`). Adding a study uses `current_runs(study, stage="main")`. The study baseline is the `StudyCondition` with `is_baseline` (of the active plan when it is set), in `services/studies.py`.
- **API (WP-17).**
  - `GET candidate-jobs/` → `GET candidate-runs/?study_id=` returns `CandidateRun {id, model_name, model_id, app_name, app_slug, study, condition, repeat_index, kind, stack, created_at, functional, running}`.
  - Task create/patch take `study_id` and `run_ids`; app add takes `{run_ids, study_id, current_only}`. `TaskListItem.experiment` → `study`; `TaskApp.job_id` → `run_id`; results `apps[].job_id` → `run_id`.
  - `apps.csv` replaces `job_id, experiment_id/_name, generation_mode, job_created_at, tester_sha256` with `run_id, study_id/_name, task_ref, prose_variant, stack, auth_mode, unit_key, run_kind, harness, run_created_at, run_fingerprint, tester_version, static_findings_loc_model`.
- **`EvalTask.experiment` keeps its v2 attribute name** (`models.py` is not mine). Services write `experiment_id` and use `studies.STUDY_FIELD` (resolved through `_meta`) for `select_related` and admin, so no human_eval file outside `models.py` names the field. `wilcoxon_p` (from `_legacy` experiment_results) is now in `human_eval/services/stats.py`.
- **Temporary test URLconf** `backend/human_eval/tests/urls_u1.py` + `tests/conftest.py` (DELETE at the U1 gate). `ROOT_URLCONF` points there for every human-eval test. It serves the project URLconf when `config.urls` imports, and otherwise an API with `config/api.py`'s auth, error handlers and mount points that holds only the human-eval routers. `config.urls` currently fails in `reports/services/study_report.py`.

Until that stamp exists on the hosted container the participant tests (`hosting.current_container(task_app)`), the portal returns `admin_credentials: null`, `admin_credentials_status: "not_measured"` even when the record exists (decision: `services/credentials.admin_credentials(run, container)`; tests in `tests/test_portal_work.py`). A container rebuilt or started with a rotated record (stamp mismatch) is also `not_measured`.

Code outside `models.py` names the field only via `services/studies.STUDY_FIELD` and `experiment_id` (pool.py, studies.py, items.py, tasks.py, analysis.py, tests): after the rename, replace `experiment_id` with `study_id` there and drop `STUDY_FIELD`; I can do that the moment the field is renamed.

No human_eval migration was added. Renaming it to `study` (one `RenameField` migration plus `experiment_id` → `study_id` in pool/studies/items/tasks/analysis/tests) is optional follow-up work for WP-01. ## U1 decisions (execution: WP-02a execution re-point), 2026-11-03

Files: every module of `backend/execution/` except `models.py`, `registry.py`, `breaker.py`, `runtime_builds.py`, `admin.py` and the migrations (unchanged; the BuildKit WIP keeps working), plus all of `backend/execution/tests/`. No migration.

- **SQL rewritten on `generation_run` / `generation_study`** (`scheduler.py`): lanes are partitioned by `coalesce(study_id, 'adhoc:'||created_by_id)` × endpoint; lane rows read `study_id, cell_id, task_version_id, slot_target_id, condition_id, repeat_index`, `pinned_served_name`/`pinned_digest` (no JSON paths), and `x.max_parallel`, `x.execution_options`, `x.scheduling`. Runs of a study are claimable while the study is **`piloting` or `running`**. `batch_id`/`app_requirement_id` are gone. Both claims (generation and eval) return `id, attempt, lane, study_id, endpoint_id, condition_id, cell_id, repeat_index` → `Claimed(run_id, lane, attempt, study_id, endpoint_id, condition_id, cell_id, repeat_index)`. Blocked reason `experiment_cap` → **`study_cap`** (from `Study.max_parallel`). Eval stays claimed by phase (`running/eval_queued`), so reference runs need no lane.
- **`capacity.py`**: `_ACTIVE_SQL` groups by `study_id` and `pinned_served_name` (`CapacitySnapshot.study_active`). `SLOT_BOUNDS["post_run_slots"] = (0, 2)` (also `ExecutorSettingsPatch`, `le=2`). The memory rule's sandbox term reads **`AGENT_SANDBOX_MEM_LIMIT_MAX`** (the compatibility `AGENT_SANDBOX_MEM_LIMIT` has no execution reader any more).
- **`sequence.py`**: policies `auto | interleaved | blocked` (v2 `grouped` removed: not a `Scheduling` choice); cells are `(condition_id, unit_id, repeat_index)`; `order_seed(study)` = `Study.order_seed`; `resident_limit(endpoint, study)` reads `study.execution_options.max_resident_models`; `condition_info` reads `StudyCondition`. WP-10's `study/order.py` may supersede it; nothing outside execution imports it.
- **`api.py`**: `enqueue_runs(runs_or_ids)` (lane from `run.kind` via `models.run.LANE_BY_KIND`, priority from `Study.priority`), **`enqueue_evaluation(runs_or_ids)`** (minimal: pending/unleased runs → `running/eval_queued`, `queued_at`, wake on commit; runs in their generation stage or being evaluated are left alone), `request_reeval(run)`, `cancel_runs(qs, scope, study=)`. v2 `requeue_legacy_jobs` (thread-era rows, batches) is deleted.
- **`transitions.py`**: `RESULT_FIELDS` = outcome, outcome_reason, usable, validity, result, metrics, error_message, tree_hash, workspace_blob, resolved_lock_blob, lock_source, registry_snapshot_at. **`usable` is derived from `outcome` on every guarded write** (`derive_usable`; queryset updates bypass `derive_fields`). `infra_retries` comes from the run's `resource_policy.content.retries.infra_retries` (default 2 without a policy). A requeue wipes the attempt (results, outcome, tree/lock/workspace blobs, **RunFile rows**, artifacts created since `started_at`) and keeps manifest and seed; the spend record keys are `cost_usd`, `list_cost_usd`, `prompt_tokens`, `completion_tokens`, `total_tokens` (`execution.prior_attempts_<key>`). **Exhausted retries end `FAILED` + `outcome=infra_error`** (`outcome_reason` = the retry reason, `usable=False`) for every reason, including `infra_interrupted` (v2 ended those COMPLETED-invalid). Cancel sets `excluded:cancelled`, `usable=False`. `maybe_complete_experiment` → **`maybe_complete_study`** (running studies only; a piloting study is never completed here; no automation hook: post-runs attached at launch become claimable). Events: `publish_run(run)` → `run:<id>` `status` + `study:<id>` `run` through `realtime.events.publish_run_event/publish_study_event` (payloads match the contracts; a test parses them). `refresh_cell(cell_id)` calls `runs.queries.refresh_current(cell)` after every terminal transition, give-up and cancel (NotImplementedError tolerated until WP-10).
- **`reconciler.py`**: a lost evaluation past `EVAL_MAX_REQUEUES` ends COMPLETED with `outcome=infra_error`, `outcome_reason=eval_lost`, `result.error` (a lost re-evaluation keeps the outcome it had). `completion_sweep` works on studies. v2 step 8 (`fast_forward_schedules`) is removed (see asks).
- **`workers.py`**: generation → `agentic.dispatch.run_stage(run)`, evaluation → `evaluation.suite.run_eval_stage(run)`; until WP-08/WP-09 land the skeleton's `NotImplementedError` is logged and the lease stays (the lost-run check requeues it as `executor_lost`, bounded by `EXECUTOR_LOST_RETRIES`). Post-runs: `claimable_post_runs()` (pending and due — a `completed`-triggered one once its study is `completed`/`stopped` — or running with an expired lease), `claim_post_run(executor_id)` (`FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED OF self`, status running, lease, `started_at`), then `study.automation.run_post_run(post_run)`; a `NotImplementedError` puts it back to pending. Schedules: `run_due_schedules()` calls `study.automation.fire_due_schedules(now)` on the schedule pool every 30 s.
- **`executor.py`**: `tick` events go to `study:<id>` for piloting/running/paused studies; counts are over the **current run per cell** (`runs.view.current_runs(study, stage)`; stage `pilot` while piloting), `cells.total` = cells of that stage. Heartbeat/shutdown/idle use `StudyPostRun`.
- **API shapes** (`api_schemas.py`, `api_views.py`): `CapacityOut.studies: [StudyCapacityRow]` (was `experiments`), `DrainingRemaining.runs` (was `jobs`), `QueueItem.{run_id, kind, study_id, cell_id}` (was `job_id`, `experiment_id`), `GET /execution/queue/?study_id=` (was `experiment_id`). `EndpointCapacity.jobs` keeps its name (the endpoint's `max_active_jobs` cap). `manage.py executor status` reports `running_runs` (was `running_jobs`).
- **Tests**: the capacity/queue/settings view tests call `api_views.router` through ninja's `TestClient` (authenticated as the user), so they do not depend on the project URLconf. Test helpers (`tests/conftest.py`): `make_run(user, model, lane=, study=, sequence=)` (sandbox → `app_agentic`, llm → `slot_single_shot` of a per-test shared slot target), `running_study(user, scheduling=, ...)`, `policy_with_infra_retries(n)`.

## U1 asks (execution)

- **WP-10 (launch)**: set `pinned_served_name`/`pinned_digest` on every run of a **local** endpoint at launch (from `manifest.model.endpoint`). The scheduler admits a local run only when the live session serves that name (an empty name is `model_not_served`), and residency counts it. Fill `Study.max_parallel` and `Study.execution_options` at launch, and call `execution.api.enqueue_runs(ids)` in `on_commit`.
- **WP-10 (automation/queries)**: `study.automation.run_post_run(post_run)` receives a **leased** `StudyPostRun` (status `running`, `lease_owner` = executor id, renewed by the heartbeat while the post pool is busy) and must end it (`completed`/`failed`, lease cleared). `fire_due_schedules(now)` is called every 30 s and must do the no-burst catch-up itself (v2 reconciler step 8 is gone). `runs.queries.refresh_current(cell)` is called by the executor after every terminal transition; it should also maintain `StudyCell.state`.
- **WP-08 (run_stage) / WP-09 (run_eval_stage)**: end each stage with `execution.transitions.finish_stage(run, OUTCOME_EVALUATE | OUTCOME_DONE | OUTCOME_FAILED | OUTCOME_INFRA_INTERRUPTED)` after setting the `RESULT_FIELDS` on the object (set `outcome`; `usable` is derived), use `guarded_save` for intermediate writes and let `LeaseLost` propagate. `infra_retry` deletes the attempt's `RunFile` rows and the `GenerationArtifact`s created since `started_at`; if you prefer keeping failed attempts' artifacts (they carry `attempt`), tell the execution owner. A re-evaluation (`execution.reeval`) ends in the previous status. The v2 infra-classification tests (offline endpoint before any call → retry, local error after a successful call → `infra_interrupted`, model 4xx → failed) were deleted with the orchestrator; please re-create them for `run_stage`.
- **WP-11**: `enqueue_evaluation` is a minimal implementation (above); block claiming, `StudyCondition.paused`, per-model/endpoint/provider concurrency, `eval_parallel` and budget reservations are not in the claim yet. `LaneRow.scheduling` is read but not used at admission (order comes from `sequence`). The warm-up body takes `num_ctx`/`keep_alive` from the live access (the v3 manifest pins no context size); `gateway_config` should send the same `num_ctx` or Ollama reloads the model.
- **WP-02b (system, integrations)**: `system/services/maintenance.py` still imports `GenerationBatch`, `services.batches.refresh_batch`, `services.result_writer.publish_status` and calls `transitions.maybe_complete_experiment`: use `GenerationRun`, `transitions.publish_run(run)` and `transitions.maybe_complete_study(study_id)`. `integrations/signals.py`'s docstring names `cancel_jobs`/`maybe_complete_experiment` (now `cancel_runs`/`maybe_complete_study`).
- **WP-17 (frontend `lib/api/{execution,types/execution}.ts`)**: the shape changes listed above (`studies`, `draining_remaining.runs`, `study_cap`, `QueueItem.run_id/kind/study_id/cell_id`, `?study_id=`, `post_run_slots` 0-2; tick/run events on `study:<id>`).
- **Lead / WP-01**: `AGENT_SANDBOX_MEM_LIMIT` (compatibility setting) is no longer read by execution; only `config/tests/test_generation_settings.py` still mentions it. `docs/BACKGROUND_SERVICES.md` / `docs/EXECUTION.md` (WP-11) still describe jobs, experiments and `post_run_slots ≤ 1`.

## U1 decisions (statistics: WP-02b statistics, final), 2026-11-03

Files: `statistics/services/{runs (new),study,variability,similarity,dependencies,dedup,aggregations,accounting,trend}.py`, `statistics/api/{views,schema}.py`, `statistics/management/commands/export_study_tables.py`; `healing_ablation.py` deleted; tests ported (`tests/helpers.py` new: `make_run`, `analyze`, `StudyBuilder`, `eval_summary`).

- **`services/runs.py`** holds the scope/label/code helpers (re-exported from `services/study.py`, so the names announced above work from either): `study_runs`, `run_labels`/`labels_by_run`/`RUN_LABEL_KEYS`, `condition_label`, `app_unit`, `normalize_path`, `code_loc(files, has_tree=)` and `load_code(runs)` -> `RunCode` (`run_views_code` for app and model scope, one query each; `loc()`/`loc_app()`; `model_paths()`). Everything reads `runs.view` only (`current_runs`, `run_views`, `run_views_code`); when WP-10 lands `runs.queries.current_runs`, switch the import in `statistics/services/runs.py` and `variability.py` (`from backend.generation.runs.view import current_runs`).
- **Measured = completed without `summary.measurement == "not_measured"`** (`study.tool_measured`), matching the analysis app's §8.8 change: a not-applicable tool is no longer a measured zero (coverage reason `not_applicable`; it drops out of `tools_common`; `code_metrics_by_run` leaves its columns `None`; `get_tool_effectiveness` counts it under `not_measured`).
- **Attribution by origin at stats time** (§8.8, Appendix A #4): an issue is the model's when its (normalized) path is in the run's model scope (`code_unique_model`, `security_unique_model`, cluster `model_scope`); `loc` = model-scope non-test source LOC, `loc_app` = whole tree; `unique_per_kloc` (per app and per group) = model code issues per model kLOC over runs with a measured LOC. `group_summary` skips runs whose `loc` is `None`.
- **Variability**: `study_variability(study, *, fingerprint="majority"|"all", sensitivity=None|"exclude_truncated")` (ValueError otherwise), `study_variability_csv(payload)`, `run_replicates(run, user)`, `model_variability(model, user)` (the four names WP-02a's views call). Selection: main-stage `current_runs`, `usable is True` only (the rest is counted in `selection.not_usable` and a note), `repeat_index < study.repeats`, majority `config_fingerprint` per (condition, unit) (tie broken by the lowest fingerprint), common (unit, repeat) cells; **ITT default** (truncated kept, `NOTE_ITT`), truncation exclusion only as the sensitivity. Interim (`study.status` not completed/cancelled/stopped) flags every block and withholds the planner. Metrics come from the `GenerationRun.metrics` contract keys (`cost_usd`, `list_cost_usd`, `total_tokens`, `requests_by_class` summed = LLM calls, `wall_s` else `duration_seconds`, `eval.agent_pass_rate` only when `eval.status == "measured"`); check agreement reads the `RunEvaluation` named by `metrics.eval.evaluation_id` (infra and flaky checks left out; one suite+tester version per cell). Billing: the manifest's `model.endpoint.cost_basis != "billed"` marks a billed $0 as billing, never a floor. Payload keys renamed: `study_id` (was `experiment_id`), cells `unit_key`/`task`/`task_title`/`run_ids`/`n_pass_known` (were `app_id`/`app_slug`/`app_name`/`job_ids`), design/conditions `n_units`, pairs `run_a`/`run_b`, `filters` = `{stage, fingerprint, sensitivity, itt, planned_only, common_cells}`, new `selection` block; the `behaviour.*` metrics and the "approximate source" notion are gone. Replicates: grouped by `config_fingerprint` only (no legacy approximate key); relations `self | retry` (same cell) `| replay` (same `run_fingerprint`) `| rerun` (ad hoc, beyond plan, or not its cell's current run) `| replicate`; member keys `run_id`, `study_id`/`study_name`, `cell_attempt`, `beyond_plan`, `outcome`, `usable`, `truncated`. Model groups: `task`, `unit_key`, `kind`, `stack`, `harness`, `studies`, `run_ids`; `n_completed_runs`.
- **Similarity**: code = model-scope non-test source (`model_source_map`, `SourceStore` via `run_views_code(..., "model")`), `ALGO_VERSION = 2`; the call probe reads `^(agent|slot)_call_\d{4}$` artifacts of each run's last `attempt`.
- **Accounting**: `PLATFORM_EVALUATORS` lists `eval-tester` (was `conformance-tester`) and `smoke`; `evaluations` = `{"suite": {executions, by_status, retests, runs, latest_by_status, deployed, not_deployed, not_measured, error, evaluated}}` (the v2 `conformance`/`smoke`/`healing_ablation` blocks are gone); `apps` = `{runs, by_status, by_outcome, usable, generated, analyzed, evaluated, deployed}`.
- **Aggregations**: `get_code_generation_stats` sums `metrics.{total_tokens,cost_usd,loc_model}` over completed runs that recorded them (`None` when none did, with `tokens_runs`/`cost_runs`/`loc_runs`), `unique_stacks` (was `unique_templates`); `get_model_comparison(user, limit, *, prompt_hash, config_fingerprint, study_id)` calls `rankings.aggregate_rankings` with the same keywords and returns `pass_rate` (was `smoke_pass_rate`).
- **API** (`/api/statistics/`): `models/?prompt_hash=&config_fingerprint=&study_id=`; `study/breakdowns/?study_id=`, `study/accounting/?study_id=` (`experiment_id`/`batch_id` gone, malformed id -> 400); `study/healing/` removed. Breakdown groups `by_task`/`by_model_task` (were `by_template`/`by_model_template`).
- **export_study_tables**: `--study <uuid>` (unknown/malformed id -> CommandError) and `--stage main|pilot` (default main; never pooled); files `issues_by_task.csv`/`issues_by_model_task.csv`; the four `healing_*.csv` files are gone; `study_summary.json.scope = {study, stage, runs}`.
- **Tests deleted** (pinned removed behaviour): `test_study.py::{test_healing_impact_pairs_outcomes, test_binomial_sign_test, test_not_applicable_skip_counts_as_measured}` (the last one replaced by `test_not_applicable_tool_is_not_measured`), `test_similarity.py::TestAgentSourceMap::{test_template_mode_subtracts_skeleton_else_is_approximate, test_single_blob_has_no_paths}` (no skeleton/blob modes in v3), `test_variability.py::{test_pass_rate_follows_the_results_tab_and_names_a_smoke_check}` (no smoke-check pass rate in v3; the v2 Results-tab `experiment_results.compute` parity is WP-10's), `test_aggregations.py::test_lines_of_code_are_source_loc` (replaced by the metrics-contract sum test). Everything else was ported to v3 factories.

## U1 decisions (reports_export: WP-02b reports + export), 2026-11-03

Files: `reports/services/{__init__,loc,generators,study_report,renderers,report_service,plan_stats (new)}.py`, `reports/management/commands/study_report.py`, `export/services/{__init__,findings,review_sample,tasks}.py`, `export/services/runs.py` (`git mv` of `jobs.py`), `export/api/views.py`, `export/management/commands/export_review_sample.py`, tests `reports/tests/{helpers (new),test_loc,test_generators,test_study_report,test_study_code_metrics,test_renderers,test_services}.py`, `export/tests/{test_export,test_review_sample}.py`.

- **`reports.services.loc`**: `source_loc(path, text) -> int` (non-blank, non-comment lines of a source file; 0 for tests, lockfiles, manifests, docs, minified/vendored/dependency paths) is the one rule; `runs/view.source_loc` now delegates to it (the package imports cleanly, so the view's file-loading fallback is dead code). Run LOC: `loc_from_view(view)` / `loc_from_run(run)` / `loc_by_run(runs)` / `loc_for_runs(runs)` read the **model** scope through RunView: `total_loc` = `RunView.loc("model")` (stored `RunFile.loc` of the source rows), backend/frontend split, `source_files`, `test_loc`/`test_files` (test files' text via `code_files("model")`). Every value is `None` without a collected tree; `loc_for_runs` sums only runs with a known LOC (`counted`), totals `None` when none. `loc_from_job`/`loc_from_result_data`/`loc_for_jobs` are gone. `reports/services/__init__` imports only `loc` eagerly; `GENERATORS`/`create_and_dispatch`/`list_reports` resolve lazily (module `__getattr__`).
- **Report types**: the `Report.Type` values are unchanged (`reports/models.py` is WP-01's), but `template_comparison` is now the **task comparison** (`config.task_slug`, optional `task_version`, `filter_models`, `treatment_hash`, `prompt_hash`; rows per `(model, treatment_hash)`; `mixed_treatments`) and `experiment_report` the **study conditions report** (`config.study_id`; per condition: label (study tables' name), `is_baseline`, `condition_hash`, `treatment_hash`, run summary, LOC, findings, dedup, stats over `current_runs(study, "main")`; `sample.{runs, beyond_plan_excluded, pilot_excluded, superseded_attempts_excluded, truncation_rule}`; `results` = `generation.study.results.study_results(study)` or `None` with a note while WP-10's stub raises `NotImplementedError`). `study_report` takes `study_id` and/or `run_ids` (`batch_id`/`experiment_id` gone). `owned_study(study_id, user)` replaces `owned_experiment` ("Study not found" for unknown/malformed/other user's).
- **Samples, ITT, attrition**: reports read `statistics.services.runs.study_runs` / `current_runs` (first usable attempt per main-stage cell; beyond-plan and pilot runs never in the sample, counted in `protocol.{beyond_plan_excluded,pilot_excluded}`), never reference runs. Truncation is `validity.truncated`; truncated runs are **trials by default** (intention-to-treat); `exclude_truncated` is the sensitivity analysis and a study plan's `exclusions.truncated` wins (generators: only when the study is locked). Runs whose `usable` is not True (infra_error, excluded) are **attrition** in the study report (`protocol.attrition_excluded`, validity `attrition`), never failures.
- **Evaluation** columns come from `RunView.latest_evaluation()` (planned suite, retests excluded): `evaluated` = status `measured` (not_measured/error = validity `infra_errors`, never a 0); `deployed` = `outcome == tested`; `pass_rate` = `result.metrics.agent_pass_rate` (0.0 for an evaluated build/start failure, as before); `functional_passed` = pass rate 1. Generator stats read the `metrics["eval"]` mirror (`runs_measured`, `runs_not_measured`, `runs_all_passed`). Spend is `metrics.cost_usd` (`None` when no run recorded any, never 0). Providers come from `RunAttempt.served.providers`.
- **Attribution by origin**: densities, weighted scores, critical/high counts, `issues_per_kloc`, `security_per_app` and the plan metrics use **model-scope** findings (`code_unique_model`, `security_unique_model`; a finding is the model's when its normalized path is in `RunView.files("model")`) over model-scope LOC; `code_unique` stays the app tree's. `tool_analysis` counts a not-applicable / not-measured result under `skipped`, never as a measured zero (`study.tool_measured`).
- **Study report changes**: `REPORT_VERSION = 3`; build-repair ablation section, protocol rows and validity check removed; `templates`→`tasks` (`funnel.by_task`, `code_metrics.by_task`, task in `per_app`); `modes`→`kinds` (`mixed_kinds`); `mixed_prompts` replaced by `mixed_fingerprints` (more than one `config_fingerprint` within a (condition, unit)); `scaffold_dependencies`→`starter_dependencies`; `plan_not_frozen`→`plan_not_locked` (locked = `Study.locked_at`); `design_extended` reports beyond-plan runs left out; `stopped_early` = study `stopped` or main-stage cells excluded as `budget_stop`/`stopped_by_rule`/`cancelled`; baseline = the `is_baseline` condition. The pure plan helpers (`PLAN_METRICS`, `declared_metrics`, `plan_multiplicity` (default now `all`)) and the paired tests (`sign_test_p`, `wilcoxon_p`, `cluster_bootstrap_ci`, `MIN_APPS_FOR_CI`) are ported into `reports/services/plan_stats.py` (no `_legacy` import). Row labels and the app unit are `statistics.services.runs.{run_labels, app_unit}` (`unit_key`), condition names `condition_label` (label, else model id).
- **Exports**: `GET /api/export/generation-runs.{csv,json}` (was `generation-jobs`; filters `status, model_id, study_id, kind, outcome`; columns `export.services.runs.RUN_HEADERS`). New `GET /api/export/runs/{run_id}/bundle.zip` (owner or staff, else 404): `manifest.json` (frozen RunManifest or `null`), `files.csv` (`path, origin, sha, loc` then `size, mode, language, is_test, excluded`; app scope, `parent` for a variant's overlaid rows), `resolved_lock.json` (`{recorded, lock_source, registry_snapshot_at, blob, lock, from_parent_run}`; `recorded: false` when no lock was resolved, never "no dependencies"), `task.json` (`task_dict()`), `run.json` and `code/<path>` (app scope). Findings exports: `generation_run_id, study_id, condition_id, condition_label, model, task, unit, kind, stack, repeat_index` and **`file_origin`** (the analysis' `tool_specific_data.file_origin`, else `RunView.files("app")`; empty for URLs); query params `generation_run_id`, `study_id`. Query count is fixed per run, not per finding (`files("app")` is one query per run; a bulk files reader would make it constant). Analysis-task exports: `generation_run_id`, `code_scope`, target `run:<id>`.
- **Review sample** (format 4): population = usable completed runs with a tree (`study_id` → the study's current runs, owner-checked; shell: `export_review_sample --study`); stratify `model`/`task`; per app `code/` (app scope), `files.csv`, `resolved_lock.json`, `REQUIREMENTS.md` (task title, chosen prose, endpoints `E<n>`, UI `U<n>`); the manifest (names the model) goes to `key/<sid>/manifest.json`; key columns `run_id, model, task, condition_label, kind, repeat_index, outcome, eval_status, eval_pass_rate, deployed, analysis_run_ids`; dependency origin `model | starter | transitive`; scoring compares with `eval_pass_rate` (a not-measured evaluation has no automated verdict).
- **Tests deleted** (pinned removed behaviour): `test_loc.py::{test_single_blob_results_have_unknown_test_code, test_loc_for_jobs_sums_test_code_only_where_it_is_known}`; `test_services.py::{test_loc_from_job_counts_non_blank_non_comment, test_loc_from_job_sums_multi_file_result_by_extension, test_loc_for_jobs_aggregates, test_job_summary_reports_truncated_jobs, test_template_comparison_excludes_truncated_trials_by_default (ITT now), test_template_comparison_single_bundle_key_not_flagged_mixed, test_generate_experiment_report_malformed_id_raises (merged), test_generate_experiment_report_per_condition_stats_and_deltas (results are WP-10's)}`; `test_renderers.py::{test_legacy_experiment_report_is_not_relabelled, test_repeats_beyond_the_plan_are_not_missing, test_legacy_study_payload_keeps_its_old_meaning, test_condition_grouped_payloads_without_by_group_use_the_group_labels, test_healing_table_shows_apps_and_runs_when_the_payload_has_both}`; `test_study_report.py::{test_stopped_early_and_extended_designs (split into the stopped-cells and beyond-plan tests), test_unknown_test_code_and_uncovered_ai_findings_are_not_zero (single-blob part; AI part kept)}` and the build-repair assertions of `test_every_table_names_a_group_alike`; `test_study_code_metrics.py::{test_export_writes_code_metrics_per_app, test_export_of_an_empty_scope_keeps_the_header}` (they test the statistics `export_study_tables` command). Every other test was ported to v3 factories (`reports/tests/helpers.py`: runs with a real collected tree, analyses, study cells, evaluations).

## U1 decisions (inference_runtime fixer, round 1), 2026-11-03

- **`inference/inventory.py` digest-drift pause (Appendix A #1):** the deleted `experiment_automation` import is gone. `_pause_for_drift` (still the `on_commit` hook) calls the new `pause_studies_for_digest_drift(model_ids)`, which sets `Study.status=paused`, `pause_reason="rule:digest_drift"` on every `piloting`/`running` study with a condition of its active plan (any condition when there is no active plan) on a changed model. The row lock re-checks the status, and a `state` event goes out on `study:<id>` after commit. `"digest_drift"` alone fails `pause_reason_validator` (`budget|quota|spend_cap|manual|infra|rule:<code>`), so the reason uses the `rule:` form. Every failure is logged with `logger.exception` and never raised into the relay caller. New tests in `inference/tests/test_inventory.py` run the real hook: running and piloting studies pause, draft and unrelated studies do not, inactive-plan conditions are ignored, and a failure is logged. **WP-11 / stop rules:** resume stays manual (`POST /studies/{id}/resume/`). Mark the affected confirmatory cells `stopped_early` if the gate needs it.
- **`LLMRequest.Source.SCAFFOLDING` → `SLOT_CALL = "slot_call"`**, with the `requests.enqueue` default `source="slot_call"`, `params.SOURCE_OFFSET["slot_call"]` and the two `gateway_wiring.py` texts (now "slot mode"). Migration `inference/0004_llmrequest_source_slot_call.py` (AlterField choices + RunPython that relabels old `scaffolding` rows, reversible). `makemigrations --check inference` is clean.
- **`runtime/management/commands/benchmark_sample_builds.py` ported to v3:** the positional args are `run_ids` (GenerationRun UUIDs, completed only). The command `prepare_build_dir`s each run once into a temp dir, makes edited copies of that dir per scenario, and builds them with `sample_builds.build_sample(run, context_dir=...)`. Neither the run nor its files are ever touched or saved. The report has `schema_version: 2` and `runs[]` `{run_id, kind, stack (build_identity), scenarios}`. A run without a tree raises a `CommandError`. `test_benchmark_sample_builds.py` was ported (6 tests).
- **`runtime/services/sample_builds.py` (runview-owned, minimal additive edit):** `build_sample(..., context_dir: Path | None = None)` copies a caller-prepared context instead of calling `prepare_build_dir`. Only the benchmark uses it. The healing rename of this file (item 2 of the fixer list) was done concurrently by the runview fixer (`SampleBuildResult.build_context`, no `healing` kwarg, shim without `HealingReport`), so I did not touch it.
- Test infra: the tests ran against a private postgres `wtpg-u1irf1` (`--shm-size=512m`, `DATABASE_URL` overridden inside `.wt-test.sh`) because the shared `/dev/shm` was full.

The WP-01 notes above that mention `--ds=...settings_u1` or `--skip-checks` are historical and no longer apply.
- **`test_identity.py` / `test_contracts.py`** use `"no_such_kind"` instead of `"scaffold"` as the invalid kind. Those tests only check that an unknown kind is rejected, so the v2 word was not needed.

Both name the v2 tables and factories on purpose, to assert that they are gone (the wipe guard and the factory shim). Exclude them explicitly:
  `grep -rniE "<pattern>" backend --include=*.py | grep -v -e "/migrations/" -e "/_legacy/" -e "generation/tests/wp01_foundation/test_migrations.py:" -e "generation/tests/wp01_foundation/test_testing_factories.py:"`
  `backend/human_eval/models.py` (the `EvalTask.experiment` field, ask under *U1 asks (human_eval)*) is the one real hit left, and someone else is changing that file at the time of writing.
- **Analysis owner:** `backend/analysis/tests/_tmp_u1_settings.py` (untracked, used only through `--ds`) star-imports the deleted `settings_u1`. Delete it and `_tmp_u1_urls.py`, which were also marked "DELETE at the U1 gate". The analysis suite runs under `config.settings.test`. Nothing collects or imports them otherwise.
- **Lead (infra, still open):** the shared `llmlab-postgres-1` `/dev/shm` is still 64M at 96%, so `.wt-test.sh` runs against it fail with `could not resize shared memory segment`. Raise its `shm_size` (for example `1g`) and restart it. That needs the user, because it is the live stack. This round used a private `wtpg-u1fxothf2` container (`llmlab/postgres:local`, `--shm-size=1g`, removed afterwards) through a scratchpad copy of `.wt-test.sh` that changes only `POSTGRES_HOST`.

## U1 asks (other: fixer round 3), 2026-11-03

- **`.wt-test.sh` can now start its own postgres, so the copies of the script are no longer needed.** `WT_PG=private` starts a throwaway `llmlab/postgres:local` container for that run only. It is named `llmlab-wtpg-<suffix>-<pid>`, runs on `llmlab_default` with `--shm-size=$WT_PG_SHM` (default `1g`) and a tmpfs at `/var/lib/postgresql` (`$WT_PG_TMPFS`, default `4g`), and creates `llmlab_wt_<suffix>`. The test container gets `POSTGRES_HOST` pointed at it, and an EXIT trap removes it. `WT_PG=shared` keeps the old behaviour. **The default `WT_PG=auto`** reads the shared container's `/dev/shm` usage with `df` (read-only) and switches to private when it is at least 90% used. Each run starts from an empty postgres, so `manage.py migrate` / `makemigrations --check` and pytest work as before. Data does not survive between runs, so use `pytest --create-db` semantics and do not rely on `--reuse-db`.
- **Lead (infra, still open, needs the user):** the shared `llmlab-postgres-1` still has `/dev/shm` at 64M/96%. It was not touched, because it is the live stack. The lasting fix is `shm_size: 1g` on the compose `postgres` service plus a restart. After that, `WT_PG=auto` goes back to using the shared postgres on its own.
